      # ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n
      # PROMPT 7: the seams between a result lane and the rest of the repo.\n
      #\n
      # The full suite above already runs these tests. This step exists\n
      # because a seam failure is a DIFFERENT KIND of failure: the lane works,\n
      # every unit passes, and the feature is still unreachable or its\n
      # configuration undocumented. Naming the contract here means the CI log\n
      # says which agreement broke instead of just "a test failed".\n
      #\n
      # Cheap greps first, so an obvious breach fails in a second rather than\n
      # after the whole suite.\n
      # ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n
      - name: Assert the result-lane seams are closed\n
        run: |\n
          set -euo pipefail\n
\n
          echo "--- robots.txt: search blocked, content crawlable ---"\n
          test -f public/robots.txt\n
          for lane in national-lottery weekly-lottery bingo-lottery pcso-lottery; do\n
            grep -qx "Disallow: /${lane}/search" public/robots.txt \\n
              || { echo "::error::robots.txt does not block /${lane}/search"; exit 1; }\n
            # A bare prefix rule would deindex the entire lane.\n
            if grep -qE "^Disallow:[[:space:]]*/${lane}/?[[:space:]]*$" public/robots.txt; then\n
              echo "::error::robots.txt blocks the whole ${lane} lane, not just its search route"\n
              exit 1\n
            fi\n
            if grep -qE "^Disallow:[[:space:]]*/${lane}/year" public/robots.txt; then\n
              echo "::error::robots.txt blocks the ${lane} year archive"\n
              exit 1\n
            fi\n
          done\n
\n
          echo "--- .env.example documents every key each lane config reads ---"\n
          node -e '\n
            const fs = require("fs");\n
            const example = fs.readFileSync(".env.example", "utf8");\n
            let failed = false;\n
            for (const lane of ["national_lottery", "weekly_lottery", "bingo_lottery", "pcso_lottery", "contact"]) {\n
              const config = fs.readFileSync("config/" + lane + ".php", "utf8");\n
              const prefix = lane === "contact" ? "CONTACT" : lane.toUpperCase();\n
              const keys = new Set(\n
                [...config.matchAll(new RegExp("env\\(\\s*[\x27\"](" + prefix + "_[A-Z0-9_]+)[\x27\"]", "g"))]\n
                  .map(m => m[1])\n
              );\n
              if (keys.size === 0) {\n
                console.error("::error::no env keys parsed out of config/" + lane + ".php");\n
                failed = true;\n
                continue;\n
              }\n
              for (const key of keys) {\n
                // A commented-out entry counts: credentials are named but valueless.\n
                if (!new RegExp("^#?\\s*" + key + "=", "m").test(example)) {\n
                  console.error("::error::" + key + " is read by config/" + lane + ".php but undocumented in .env.example");\n
                  failed = true;\n
                }\n
                // A credential must never ship with a value.\n
                if (/(TOKEN|SECRET|KEY)$/.test(key) && new RegExp("^" + key + "=.+", "m").test(example)) {\n
                  console.error("::error::" + key + " has a value in .env.example");\n
                  failed = true;\n
                }\n
              }\n
            }\n
            process.exit(failed ? 1 : 0);\n
          '\n
\n
          echo "--- /contact must stay crawlable ---"\n
          if grep -qE "^Disallow:[[:space:]]*/contact" public/robots.txt; then\n
            echo "::error::robots.txt blocks /contact, which is ordinary public content"\n
            exit 1\n
          fi\n
\n
          echo "--- no competitor string in any public surface ---"\n
          # The reference pages that motivated these lanes may be named in\n
          # audit notes and prompts. They must never appear in anything that\n
          # ships. Scoped to production source so a research note in a report\n
          # file does not fail the build.\n
          if grep -rniE "thailotto" \\n
               app config routes lang public database \\n
               resources/views resources/css resources/js \\n
               2>/dev/null | grep -v "^resources/views/vendor/"; then\n
            echo "::error::a competitor reference reached production source"\n
            exit 1\n
          fi\n
\n
          echo "--- guest navigation links both public lanes ---"\n
          for name in national-lottery.index weekly-lottery.index bingo-lottery.index pcso-lottery.index contact; do\n
            grep -q "route('${name}')" resources/views/layouts/app.blade.php \\n
              || { echo "::error::guest navigation has no link to ${name}"; exit 1; }\n
          done\n
\n
          echo "Seam contracts hold."\n
\n
      - name: Seam and lane test suites (named, so a failure says which)\n

  # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n
  # PROMPT 6: the Weekly Lottery integrity crate.\n
  #\n
  # A SEPARATE JOB, not extra steps on the PHP job, for two reasons. It needs a\n
  # Rust toolchain the PHP job has no use for, and the two must be able to fail\n
  # independently: a broken canonicalizer and a broken Blade template are\n
  # different problems and should not hide behind one red cross.\n
  #\n
  # --locked everywhere. The lock file is committed, and a CI run that silently\n
  # resolved a different dependency tree would not be testing what ships - which\n
  # matters more than usual for a component whose whole job is producing a\n
  # deterministic hash.\n
  # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n
  rust-integrity:\n
    name: Rust integrity verifier\n
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n
\n
    defaults:\n
      run:\n
        working-directory: security/weekly-result-integrity\n
\n
    steps:\n
      - name: Checkout\n
        uses: actions/checkout@v4\n
\n
      - name: Setup Rust\n
        uses: dtolnay/rust-toolchain@stable\n
        with:\n
          components: rustfmt\n
\n
      - name: Cache cargo registry and build output\n
        uses: actions/cache@v4\n
        with:\n
          path: |\n
            ~/.cargo/registry\n
            ~/.cargo/git\n
            security/weekly-result-integrity/target\n
          key: cargo-${{ runner.os }}-${{ hashFiles('security/weekly-result-integrity/Cargo.lock') }}\n
          restore-keys: cargo-${{ runner.os }}-\n
\n
      - name: Formatting\n
        run: cargo fmt --check\n
\n
      - name: Type check\n
        run: cargo check --locked --all-targets\n
\n
      - name: Tests\n
        run: cargo test --locked\n
\n
      - name: Assert the crate cannot reach the network or a database\n
        run: |\n
          set -euo pipefail\n
          # The "no network" property is enforced by ABSENCE of any such\n
          # dependency. tests/integrity.rs already asserts this against\n
          # Cargo.lock; this repeats it against the RESOLVED tree so a\n
          # transitive addition cannot slip in behind a direct one.\n
          tree=$(cargo tree --locked --edges normal --prefix none)\n
          for crate in tokio reqwest hyper curl ureq socket2 mio rustls native-tls sqlx diesel postgres tokio-postgres; do\n
            if printf '%s\n' "$tree" | grep -qE "^${crate} v"; then\n
              echo "::error::forbidden dependency in the integrity crate: ${crate}"\n
              exit 1\n
            fi\n
          done\n
          echo "No networking or database crate is present."\n
\n
      - name: Release build (the binary Laravel invokes)\n
        run: cargo build --release --locked\n
\n
      - name: Smoke test the stdin/stdout contract\n
        run: |\n
          set -euo pipefail\n
          # Leading zeros must survive, and a malformed value must be refused\n
          # with exit code 2 rather than repaired.\n
          ok=$(printf '%s' '{"draw_reference":"WK-20260918","draw_date":"2026-09-18","first_6":"001234","three_ball":"049","two_ball":"09","source_identifier":"CI","parser_version":"1"}' \\n
            | ./target/release/weekly-result-integrity)\n
          echo "$ok"\n
          printf '%s\n' "$ok" | grep -q '"status":"INTEGRITY_HASH_ONLY"'\n
          printf '%s\n' "$ok" | grep -q '"acceptable":true'\n
\n
          set +e\n
          printf '%s' '{"draw_reference":"WK-20260918","draw_date":"2026-09-18","first_6":"1234","parser_version":"1"}' \\n
            | ./target/release/weekly-result-integrity\n
          code=$?\n
          set -e\n
          test "$code" -eq 2\n
          echo "Malformed payload refused with exit code 2."\n
\n
